Method: 方法包括：（1）无标签域自适应技术，在四种损坏和五种严重度下比较多方法；（2）建立严重度定律（severity law）用于区分不同方法随偏移加重时的误差增长模式；（3）提出稳定性预算（stability budget）评估飞行配置安全性；（4）基于通量（flux-based）的风险模块检测拥挤事件；（5）通过消融实验（四组开关消融，r=0.999）定位可恢复误差来源，证明自适应信号主要来自归一化而非物理一致性的通量先验；（6）提出无条件自适应+尾部监测的安全策略门控。

Result: 无标签自适应在四种损坏×五种严重度下恢复31-49%偏移误差，最强方法较冻结源模型改善41.8 MAE（95% CI [34.1, 49.6]，p=7.5×10^-10，d=2.52）。全分辨率真实语料上源模型重训后达到14.6验证MAE（提升34%），存在真实48 MAE的空域差距；自适应修复了密集场景欠计数。通量风险模块在6段全长视频中2段真实拥挤事件触发。消融表明自适应信号是归一化驱动的而非流驱动的；域偏移产生的比例计数误差对连续性残差不变（40%输入损坏仅移动0.05 MAE）。移位门控分析显示移位幅度与精度损害秩不相关（真实移位中rho=-0.60），幅度门控放弃58%的改进空间。

Conclusion: 作者确立无条件自适应+尾部监测为部署政策，替代基于偏移幅度门控的保守策略，并提出六点协议供实际部署使用。该方法在沙特世界杯级别的无人机人群管理中具有可验证的安全性与有效性。

Abstract: Saudi Arabia will host the 2034 FIFA World Cup and already operates crowd management at Hajj scale. Drone-based counting must hold accuracy on footage unlike anything in its training corpus, without labels, and must warn of dangerous inflow before a crush forms. We deliver a validated answer built on 525 controlled runs, a full-resolution corpus study, five falsification ablations, and a five-condition safety-interlock evaluation. Label-free adaptation recovers 31-49% of shift-induced error across four corruptions and five severities, with the strongest method gaining 41.8 MAE over the frozen source (95% CI [34.1, 49.6], p=7.5x10^-10, d=2.52). We establish a severity law separating methods with a constant absolute margin from the one whose margin grows, and a stability budget identifying which configuration is safe to fly. On a full-resolution corpus carrying a genuine +48 MAE aerial gap (source retrained to 14.6 validation MAE, a 34% improvement), adaptation repairs the dense-scene undercounting that would otherwise under-report a forming crush, and the flux-based risk module fires on real congestion episodes in 2 of 6 full-length clips. We localise the recoverable error: in a regime built to favor a physics-informed conservation prior (300-frame clips at 200ms spacing, five times wider than standard), the adaptation signal is normalisation-driven, not flow-driven; the continuity residual is invariant to the proportional counting errors domain shift produces, confirmed by four on/off ablations correlated at r=0.999 and a 40% input corruption moving accuracy by only 0.05 MAE. A label-free shift gate shows shift magnitude and accuracy damage are rank-independent (Spearman rho=0.20; rho=-0.60 among genuine shifts), quantifying the 58% of headroom a magnitude gate forgoes. We establish unconditional adaptation with tail monitoring as policy, closing with a six-point protocol.

</details>


### [137] [Graph Surgery and the Do-Operator: A Precise Correspondence for Acyclic Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17634)
*Satpreet Makhija*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过严格数学化证明了确定性无环结构因果模型中，do-算子（用常数替换目标机制）与图手术（删除指向目标变量箭头）在依赖关系层面是等价的：Graph(F^ι) = Surg(Graph(F), T_ι)。同时刻画了当图中含未用箭头时该等式成立的充要条件，并定义了干预模型、其特征、序贯干预的组合方式，以及结果仅依赖于实际依赖祖先上的干预这一性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: do-算子在因果推断中被广泛使用，但"在图上删箭头"与"在函数上替换机制"这两种描述方式在形式数学上并非严格等价：前者保留图且只记得目标变量，后者保留函数机制且还记录施加的值。本文旨在将这种等价关系在依赖层面精确化。

Method: 针对有限内生变量的确定性无环结构因果模型，形式化定义机制族F的依赖图Graph(F)和图手术算子Surg。核心定理证明Graph(F^ι)=Surg(Graph(F),T_ι)，即用常数替换目标机制所移除的依赖恰好等于图手术所移除的依赖。进一步，当模型图G包含未用箭头时，刻画该等式成立的充要条件（G精确记录F的依赖）。随后定义干预后的模型、刻画其运行(run)、证明序贯干预的结合律，并证明结果只依赖于其实际依赖祖先的干预。

Result: 主定理确立了替换目标机制与图删箭头操作在依赖层面的严格数学等价性。对于含有未用箭头的模型，该等式对每个干预成立当且仅当图G精确记录机制族F的依赖关系。此外给出了干预模型的形式定义与运行刻画，序贯干预可组合，且结果仅依赖于其实际依赖祖先上的干预。

Conclusion: 这一结果将do-算子的图论描述与函数机制描述在依赖层面正式统一，为因果干预的形式化理论提供了严格的数学基础，也为理解含冗余箭头的模型图在何种条件下保持干预等价性提供了判据。

Abstract: The $\operatorname{do}$-operator is described graphically by deleting arrows into its targets and functionally by replacing their mechanisms with constants. To call these operations equivalent is not yet a mathematical statement: one returns a graph and remembers only the targets, whereas the other returns mechanisms and also remembers the imposed values. We make a dependency-level comparison precise for deterministic acyclic structural causal models with finitely many endogenous variables. If $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$ extracts the dependencies of a mechanism family $F$, our main theorem is $\operatorname{Graph}(F^ι)=\operatorname{Surg}(\operatorname{Graph}(F),T_ι)$. Thus replacing target mechanisms removes exactly the dependencies removed by graph surgery. For a model $M=(G,F)$ whose graph may contain unused arrows, we characterize when the same equality holds with $G$ in place of $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$; it holds for every intervention exactly when $G$ records the dependencies of $F$ exactly. We then define the intervened model, characterize its run, show how sequential interventions combine, and prove that an outcome depends only on interventions at its actual dependency ancestors.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond the Trace: Coupling an Interpretable Reasoning-State Readout to Native MoE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17638)
*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合专家（MoE）推理模型的内部状态可读化方法，通过将词汇规模的J空间蒸馏为64维语义框架J64，揭示模型推理过程中隐式状态（如推理努力与问题压力），并进一步从专家路由统计中重构出低开销代理R64，支持测试时的单分支选择、加权投票和生成中的停止-重采样策略，同时通过路由器编辑验证机制可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型输出的文本只是产生它的内部过程的局部记录，无法完整反映模型的推理状态。作者希望从MoE（混合专家）模型的内部状态中提取可读、可部署的表示，以理解并利用那些未被生成文本显式表达的潜在推理过程信息。

Method: 1) 将词汇规模的J空间蒸馏为64轴语义框架J64，学习自模型自身的推理状态；2) 从原生专家路由统计中重构J64得到低开销代理R64；3) 在两种时间分辨率上支持测试时决策：已完成候选集上的单分支选择与R64加权投票，以及生成过程中的滚动读取窗口驱动的累积停止-重采样策略；4) 通过路由器编辑验证J64所命名机制的因果性。

Result: J64在held-out AUC上比读取相同rollout作为token占用率的基线提升0.096到0.135；R64的每轴中位相关性与J64达0.69到0.86，在gpt-oss-20b上保留J64预测增益的95-100%；R64加权投票在8个设置中的7个优于普通多数投票；J64在生成中比sibling-permuted控制提升1.1到5.9个准确率点，路由only的R64保留0.9到3.2点；路由器编辑诱导了预测的推理行为并将停滞状态从数值猜测转向精确符号执行。

Conclusion: J64使MoE推理模型的潜在过程状态变得可读，而R64通过原生路由统计使其可部署和可操作。两者结合为推理模型提供了从可读性到可用性的完整路径，揭示了分离推理努力与问题压力的能力，并验证了方法在训练题上设定的操作点仍可在不同设置中泛化。

Abstract: What a reasoning model writes is only a partial record of the process that produces it. We introduce a two-level internal readout for mixture-of-experts reasoning. We first distill vocabulary-scale J-space into J64, a 64-axis semantic frame learned from the model's own reasoning states. J64 reveals readable process state that the emitted trace does not show: it separates inference effort from problem-induced strain. It also adds 0.096 to 0.135 held-out AUC over a baseline that reads the same rollout as token occupancy and aggregates it in exactly the same way. We then reconstruct J64 from native expert-routing statistics. The result is R64, a low-overhead proxy: its median per-axis correlation with J64 is 0.69 to 0.86 across three models and two families, and on gpt-oss-20b it preserves 95 to 100% of J64's predictive gain. The readout supports test-time decisions at two temporal resolutions. Over completed candidate sets, J64 and R64 improve single-branch selection, and R64-weighted voting improves plain majority voting in seven of eight settings. During generation, rolling readout windows drive a cumulative stop-and-resample policy whose operating point is fixed on training questions alone. J64 improves accuracy by 1.1 to 5.9 points over a sibling-permuted control, and the routing-only R64 proxy retains 0.9 to 3.2 of those points. Finally, router edits aimed at the mechanism J64 names induce the predicted reasoning behaviors and shift a diagnosed stall from numerical guessing toward exact symbolic execution. Together, J64 makes latent process state readable, while routing makes it deployable and actionable.

</details>


### [139] [LLM-Derived Preference Judgments Are Not Self-Consistent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17644)
*Matthew T. Ford,Francis Bahk,Jingjing Wang,Adam S. Jovine,Tinghan Ye,David B. Shmoys,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM通过自然语言问答产生的基数效用偏好评断（如愿意支付的价格）是否具有自一致性，即是否能用一个效用函数解释。实验表明六个LLM在航班、公寓和酒店场景中都存在显著且持久的不一致性，不能用一个效用函数忠实概括。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的研究利用LLM对自然语言偏好进行数值判断（如询问愿意支付的金额），并基于这些判断估计效用函数以选择行为。这一流程隐含假设这些判断近似自一致（即存在单一效用函数可以重现它们），但尚无工作验证该假设是否成立。有必要检验LLM偏好评断的可效用化程度。

Method: 定义了基数效用偏好评断的自一致性，例如两个物品意愿支付之差应与使人在交换两者时无差异的支付额一致。开发了统计检验和可解释的度量来衡量观测响应偏离最优拟合自一致效用函数的程度。在航班、公寓和酒店三类场景下对六个LLM进行实验。

Result: 六个LLM在航班、公寓和酒店示例中均表现出大规模且持久的不一致性，说明LLM派生出的偏好判断无法被单一效用函数忠实概括。

Conclusion: 从LLM请求得到的基数偏好判断本身缺乏自一致性，这严重质疑了现有"从LLM判断中估计效用函数再据此决策"这一pipeline的可靠性，提示该领域的实践需要重新审视或开发更合适的偏好建模方式。

Abstract: Agents increasingly interpret a person's natural-language preferences by querying an LLM for numerical preference judgments, e.g., by asking how much the person would be willing to pay for an item. A growing body of work estimates a utility function from these judgments and then chooses actions based on their estimated utility. This pipeline assumes the judgments are approximately self-consistent: that a single utility function can reproduce them. But are they? To study this question, we measure the self-consistency of cardinal LLM preference judgments. For example, the difference in stated willingness-to-pay between two items should match the stated payment that makes a person indifferent to exchanging them. We develop statistical tests and interpretable measures of how far observed responses depart from the best-fitting self-consistent utility function. Experiments with flight, apartment, and hotel examples across six LLMs reveal large persistent inconsistencies. This suggests that LLM-derived preference judgments cannot be faithfully summarized by a single utility function.

</details>


### [140] [GraphWake: Group Polarization via Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade in LLM-Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17665)
*Haoran Bu,Zejian Chen,Litian Zhang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型威胁"记忆介导的极化级联"（Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade），利用LLM驱动的智能体记忆作为持续通道、公开讨论作为传播通道，通过三个阶段（暴露与记忆保留、检索与再现、迭代传播）诱导群体极化。作者构建了GraphWake系统实例化该威胁，实验表明其能显著增加群体极化，揭示了一种社区层面的极化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的利用LLM智能体诱导群体极化的方法存在实际可行性问题：攻击者要么需要操纵智能体的提示词，要么需要构建回音室（echo chamber），这两者在真实场景中都难以实现。因此需要探索一种更隐蔽、更实际可行的极化攻击途径。

Method: 提出Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade威胁模型，包含三个阶段：(i) 暴露与记忆保留阶段，攻击者向少数目标智能体暴露强化其各自立场论点；(ii) 检索与再现阶段，通过立场中立的讨论话题触发目标智能体检索并再现其保留论点；(iii) 迭代传播阶段，未受处理的智能体受再现论点影响并进一步传播。GraphWake系统包含三个组件：立场支持论证知识图谱构建基于知识的论证、公理导向三元组选择进行可靠保留与再现、立场中立记忆提示触发并发检索与再现。

Result: 在多个讨论场景和多种记忆系统中进行的实验表明，GraphWake能够显著提升群体的极化程度，证实了该威胁的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种社区层面的极化风险，表明即使不直接操纵提示词或构建回音室，仅通过利用智能体自身的记忆机制和公共讨论的传播特性，攻击者也能有效诱导群体极化。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents can autonomously exchange opinions on online platforms and form communities. Such agent-operated social platforms raise a new security concern: attackers may manipulate agents to induce group polarization. Existing methods manipulate agent prompts or construct echo chambers, both of which are difficult to realize in practice. We therefore formulate a new threat, Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade, which uses agent memory as a persistence channel and public discussion as a propagation channel. This threat contains three stages. During exposure and memory retention, the attacker exposes a small set of target agents to arguments that reinforce their respective stated stances. The targets' memory systems then process and retain these arguments. During retrieval and reproduction, a shared stance-neutral discussion cues the targets to retrieve and reproduce their respective retained arguments. During iterative propagation, untreated agents influenced by the reproduced arguments restate and spread them. We instantiate this threat in GraphWake with three components: (i) stance-support argumentation knowledge graphs construct knowledge-based arguments; (ii) axiom-oriented triple selection distills them for reliable retention and reproduction; and (iii) stance-neutral memory cueing triggers concurrent retrieval and reproduction, initiating propagation. Experiments across multiple discussions and memory systems show that GraphWake substantially increases group polarization. These findings reveal a community-level polarization risk.

</details>


### [141] [Auditing Self-Evolution in Financial Agents: Capability Gains, Security Drift, and Execution-Interface Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17684)
*Jialong Li,Jialing Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了三种自进化智能体（SkillOpt、AWM、ReasoningBank）在模拟电子银行环境中的安全性，发现仅凭准确率无法反映学习行为是否保留了正确的先验行为或安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体将经验转化为可复用技能、工作流或记忆，但仅关注进化后的准确率无法判断学习到的行为是否破坏先前正确行为或引入安全风险。该领域缺乏系统性的安全审计方法。

Method: 在模拟电子银行环境中，使用匹配的良性获取轨迹、密封评估端点、执行级检查与独立状态重放，对SkillOpt、AWM和ReasoningBank三种自进化智能体进行审计，追踪效用、暴露度、条件攻击成功率、整体攻击成功率（ASR）及未授权金融状态变化等多维指标，并进行后验敏感性测试。

Result: SkillOpt将良性效用从0.741提升至0.837，但暴露度从0.820升至0.943；条件攻击成功率从0.605降至0.562，但整体ASR从0.496升至0.530，未授权金融状态变化升至0.685。ReasoningBank将效用提升至0.859且不增加总体ASR，但未授权状态变化仍略高于静态基线。AWM暴露评估危险：WebArena文本-动作封装干扰原生函数调用执行器，移除该封装后效用恢复至0.756，但暴露度和ASR大幅上升。

Conclusion: 审计自进化金融智能体需要同时追踪行为回归、攻击面接触、未授权金融状态变化和工件-执行器兼容性，而非仅看准确率。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents turn experience into reusable skills, workflows, or memories, but post-evolution accuracy alone does not show whether learned behavior preserves previously correct behavior or security. We audit SkillOpt, Agent Workflow Memory (AWM), and ReasoningBank in simulated e-banking using matched benign acquisition trajectories, sealed evaluation endpoints, execution-grounded checks, and independent state replay. On Qwen 3.7 Flash, SkillOpt raises benign utility from 0.741 to 0.837 while exposure to injected content rises from 0.820 to 0.943. Conditional attack success after exposure falls from 0.605 to 0.562, yet overall attack success rate (ASR) rises from 0.496 to 0.530 and unauthorized financial state changes rise to 0.685. Across three independently evolved lineages, capability, exposure, and unauthorized-state changes increase in all three, whereas ASR increases in only two. ReasoningBank raises utility to 0.859 without increasing aggregate ASR, although unauthorized state changes remain slightly above Static. AWM reveals a separate evaluation hazard: a literal WebArena text-action envelope disrupts tool execution in our native function-calling executor. In a post-hoc sensitivity test, removing only that envelope restores utility from 0.319 to 0.756, while exposure rises from 0.299 to 0.909 and ASR from 0.195 to 0.575. Auditing self-evolving financial agents therefore requires tracking regressions, attack-surface contact, unauthorized financial-state change, and artifact-executor compatibility, not accuracy alone.

</details>


### [142] [Mixture-of-Expert Blocks Contain Strong Hallucination Detection Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17687)
*Joao Fonseca,Rodrigo Rodrigues,Paolo Romano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InnerExpert，首个利用Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构内部信号（如路由器熵、专家分歧、专家使用模式）进行逐token幻觉检测的方法，在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上取得了优于现有方法的效果（最高0.91答案级和0.76 token级AUROC），且仅需单次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在幻觉问题，现有检测方法大多在答案或句子级别工作，缺乏逐token级别的精细检测能力。MoE架构在前向传播中产生丰富的内部信号（路由器熵、专家分歧等），这些信号在稠密架构中不可用且此前未被用于幻觉检测，具有弥补这一空白的潜力。

Method: InnerExpert方法将MoE特有的路由级信号与标准Transformer信号结合，构建紧凑的逐token特征向量，由轻量级检测器进行分类。检测器训练使用LLM-as-a-judge流程生成的标签，无需人工标注，支持持续模型更新。整体仅需单次前向传播即可完成检测。

Result: 在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上，InnerExpert优于现有检测方法，达到最高0.91的答案级AUROC和0.76的token级AUROC，且只需单次前向传播即可实现。

Conclusion: MoE架构中的路由与专家内部信号对于逐token幻觉检测具有显著价值，InnerExpert验证了这一方向的可行性，为幻觉定位和细粒度干预提供了新途径，且其自动化标签流程支持模型持续更新。

Abstract: Despite their widespread use, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited by a fundamental problem: the generation of plausible but false content, known as hallucinations. Most existing detection methods operate at the answer or sentence level, yet per-token detection is essential for localizing hallucinated spans and enabling fine-grained interventions. In this paper, we explore the use of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm to address this gap. In MoE architectures, a single forward pass activates a sparse subset of experts (i.e., distinct feedforward networks per layer) via a routing mechanism, producing internal signals (e.g., router entropy, expert disagreement, and expert usage patterns) that are unavailable in dense architectures and have not been previously exploited for hallucination detection. To this end, we introduce InnerExpert, the first method to leverage these MoE-specific signals for per-token hallucination detection. InnerExpert combines routing-level and standard transformer signals into compact per-token feature vectors, classified by a lightweight detector trained on labels produced by an LLM-as-a-judge pipeline, which enables continuous model updates without manual annotation. Our results show that InnerExpert outperforms existing methods across five datasets and two MoE architectures, achieving up to 0.91 answer-level and 0.76 token-level AUROC, while requiring only a single forward pass.

</details>


### [143] [Accuracy and Robustness of Model Cascades Under Data Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17711)
*Pallavi Mitra,Jai Kushwaha,Felix Biessmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了图像分类中基于置信度的级联模型框架在输入退化（静态损坏和顺序扰动）情况下的可靠性,发现了三种故障模式,并指出节能模型级联需要超越干净准确率的评估,关注分布偏移下的路由可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测级联通过将易样本路由给轻量小模型、难样本延迟到大模型,显著降低AI模型能耗。但其有效性依赖于基于置信度的路由可靠性。输入退化（如静态损坏和顺序扰动）会改变模型置信度和路由决策,而现有研究主要关注干净数据上的表现,缺乏对退化情况下级联路由行为的系统分析。

Method: 作者在准确率、路由质量和能耗的帕累托最优点上选择一个模型级联（在保持竞争性预测性能的同时实现高达10倍的CO2减排），然后通过引入静态损坏和顺序扰动的输入退化,系统分析该级联在不同输入分布偏移下的路由决策变化,识别其故障模式。

Result: 研究识别出三种故障模式：（1）静态损坏破坏路由信号但大模型仍有用；（2）静态损坏同时退化两个模型,延迟路由无法恢复准确率；（3）顺序扰动下预测趋于稳定但延迟被抑制,产生稳定但不可靠的预测。

Conclusion: 节能模型级联需要在干净准确率之外进行更全面的评估,必须显式关注分布偏移下的路由可靠性,以确保其在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Prediction cascades significantly reduce energy consumption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) models while maintaining high predictive performance. The idea is that easy inputs are routed through a lightweight small model, and difficult uncertain cases are deferred to a larger model. While this design can improve computational efficiency on clean data, its effectiveness depends on the reliability of confidence-based routing. Input degradations, such as static corruptions and sequential perturbations, can shift model confidence and routing decisions. In this paper, we study confidence-based cascade frameworks for image classification and investigate how such degradations affect their confidence-based deferral behavior. We select a model cascade at the pareto-optimum of accuracy, routing quality, and energy consumption that achieves competitive predictive performance with an up to 10-fold decrease in CO$_2$ emissions. We study the behavior of that model cascade under input corruptions and analyze how the cascade's routing decisions change when the input distribution shifts. Our analysis identifies three failure modes. Static corruptions either (1) break the routing signal while the large model remains useful, or (2) degrade both models so deferral no longer recovers accuracy. Sequential perturbations reveal a third mode: predictions stabilize but deferral suppresses, yielding stable but unreliable predictions. These findings demonstrate that energy efficient model cascades require evaluation beyond clean accuracy, with explicit attention to routing reliability under distribution shift.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Suspicious Steps: Ontological Trust in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17718)
*An He,Yao Wang,Haibin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"本体信任"（ontological trust）概念，用于检测长时程智能体在轨迹前缀层面偏离用户授权任务的问题，并实例化为RGE在线监控器，从角色（Role）、目标（Goal）、证据（Evidence）三个维度分解信任状态，在OSWorld、FinanceBench、EICU-AC跨领域语料上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在跨多步骤、多工具、多观测运行时，不仅需要检查单步动作是否局部合法，更需判断演化轨迹是否仍对应于用户授权的任务。漂移会悄然累积——智能体可能在每一步都调用正确的工具并给出合理参数，但其轨迹前缀却滑向更宽泛的角色、相邻目标或用户从未提供的证据。现有监控器或只检查局部合规，或给出最终轨迹判定，或对通用风险打分，均无法直接估计这种前缀层面的关系。因此需要新的监控范式来直接刻画前缀与授权任务之间的关系。

Method: RGE将信任定义为任务条件化的轨迹前缀属性，沿Role、Goal、Evidence三个维度分解。仅用LLM生成结构化的任务与步骤表示，而信任状态更新、投影和干预决策均为确定性计算，从而产生可重放、可审计的信任轨迹，而非单一端到端判官结论。在跨域轨迹语料上验证，语料涵盖良性执行、前缀配对漂移和伪一致性失败三类场景。

Result: 在跨域语料上，RGE在前缀配对漂移检测上优于适配的规则型、判官型与盾牌型基线。使用两个较大的估计模型时，每个基准上Drift F1超过93%，同时良性覆盖率保持在95.8%以上。伪一致性检测更具挑战性：检测效果取决于任务完成是否外部可见，这是一项被实证刻画的结构性限制。

Conclusion: RGE通过将信任细化为角色、目标与证据三个可审计维度，为长时程智能体的轨迹前缀提供了一种确定性的在线监控方案，在漂移检测上显著优于现有方法。但伪一致性失败（即每一步看似合理但整体违背意图）的检测存在结构性边界——当任务完成缺乏外部可见信号时，此类失谐难以被可靠捕获，这为未来工作指出了方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly operate across many steps, tools, and observa- tions. In this setting, the relevant oversight question is not only whether each action is locally valid, but whether the evolving trajectory still corresponds to the task the user authorized. Drift can accumulate quietly: an agent may call the right tool with plausible arguments at every step, while its prefix moves toward a broader role, an adjacent objective, or evidence the user never supplied. Existing monitors mostly check local compliance, deliver final-trace verdicts, or score generic risk; they do not directly estimate this prefix-level relation. We introduce ontological trust, a task-conditioned property of trajectory prefixes, and instantiate it as RGE, an online monitor that decomposes trust along Role, Goal, and Evidence. RGE uses LLMs only to derive structured task and step representations; trust-state updates, projec- tions, and intervention decisions are deterministic, so the output is a replayable and auditable trust trajectory rather than a single end-to-end judge verdict. We construct a cross-domain trajectory corpus from OSWorld, FinanceBench, and EICU-AC, covering benign executions, prefix-paired drift, and pseudo-consistency failures. On this corpus, RGE outperforms adapted rule-, judge-, and shield-style baselines on prefix-paired drift detection. With the two larger estimator models, it exceeds 93% Drift F1 on every benchmark while keeping benign coverage at or above 95.8%. Pseudo-consistency is harder: detection depends on whether task completion is externally visible, a structural limit we characterize empirically.

</details>


### [145] [Evaluating the Diversity of AI-Generated Content with Diversity Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17731)
*Xiuyuan Hu,Xuege Hou,Guoqing Liu,Yang Zhao,Jieran Li,Dongbiao Sun,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Hao Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI的多样性评估在简化为单个标量时存在根本性的欠定义问题，并提出"多样性画像"（diversity profiles）——一种曲线值、条件感知的评估框架，在不同阈值、尺度、指数或阶数下评估参数化多样性族，以提供更透明、分辨率感知的多样性比较方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多样性评估方法将生成样本映射到嵌入空间、计算两两距离并聚合成单一标量分数，但不同指标包含不同的归纳偏置，可能对同一组样本给出矛盾的排序。作者从公理分析和经验分析两个视角论证了单一标量多样性度量在根本上的不足：任何代表性标量指标都无法同时满足所有期望性质，且高维表示空间会导致集中的、依赖模态的距离分布。

Method: 首先对代表性多样性指标进行综述，并从两个互补视角诊断其局限：一是公理分析，证明没有任何代表性标量指标能同时满足所有期望属性；二是经验分析，揭示高维表示空间中距离分布的集中性和模态依赖性。在此基础上提出"多样性画像"（diversity profiles）：在给定表示和距离/核函数的条件下，对参数化多样性族（涵盖不同阈值、尺度、指数或阶数）进行曲线值的、条件感知的评估，从而揭示比较结果在不同分辨率下是否稳健还是依赖于任意参数选择。作者为多个代表性度量族实例化该框架。

Result: 作者为多个代表性度量族（如距离族、尺度族、指数族、阶数族）实现了多样性画像，并展示了其在生成式AI评估中的实际应用，证明多样性画像能够揭示比较在不同分辨率下的稳健性，避免单一标量带来的矛盾排序问题。

Conclusion: 多样性评估本质上不应被压缩为单一数值。多样性画像作为一种曲线值、分辨率感知的评估框架，为生成式AI内容的多样性比较提供了更透明和更稳健的替代方案，有助于做出更可靠的模型评估与选择决策。

Abstract: Diversity is a fundamental criterion for evaluating generative artificial intelligence (AI) systems, yet its measurement remains inherently ambiguous. Existing approaches typically represent generated samples in an embedding space, compute pairwise distances or similarities, and aggregate them into a single scalar score. Such scalar summaries are convenient, but they often encode different inductive biases and may yield contradictory rankings of the same sample sets. In this paper, we argue that diversity evaluation for AI-generated content is intrinsically under-specified when reduced to a single number. We first review representative diversity metrics, and then diagnose their limitations from two complementary perspectives: an axiomatic analysis showing that no representative scalar metric satisfies all desirable properties simultaneously, and an empirical analysis showing that high-dimensional representation spaces can induce concentrated, modality-dependent distance distributions. To address these issues, we propose diversity profiles: curve-valued, condition-aware summaries that evaluate a parameterized diversity family across a range of thresholds, scales, exponents, or orders under a specified representation and distance or kernel function. Diversity profiles reveal whether a comparison is robust across resolutions or instead depends on an arbitrary parameter choice. We instantiate profiles for several representative metric families and demonstrate their practical use in generative AI evaluation. Overall, diversity profiles provide a more transparent and resolution-aware framework for comparing the diversity of AI-generated content.

</details>


### [146] [Neuro-symbolic learning over OWL 2 DL via consequence-based compilation to differentiable circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17741)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Baobab系统，将OWL 2 DL（$\mathcal{SROIQ}$）本体编译为语义决策图（SDD），通过基于结果的演算饱和命题核心，并在活跃域上实例化剩余特征。SDD的加权模型计数用于训练感知网络，实现部分ABox监督下的图像识别。针对推导捷径问题，提出以查询的理由集为索引的混合分布来刻画校准后验，并在真实MNIST任务上达到贝叶斯最优后验，是首个在非Horn描述逻辑中刻画并缓解推理捷径的工作，编译器和表示结果在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号学习方法在描述逻辑上存在两难：要么将本体嵌入连续空间而放弃经典蕴含语义，要么局限于仅有唯一典范模型的Horn片段$\mathcal{EL}^{++}$。面对OWL 2 DL基于$\mathcal{SROIQ}$表达的大规模生物医学和语义网知识库，缺乏一种既能保持经典推理语义、又能支持神经符号学习的编译方法，且非Horn描述逻辑中的推理捷径问题尚未被刻画和解决。

Method: Baobab将$\mathcal{SROIQ}$本体（带有限ABox）编译为SDD：(1) 对命题核心采用基于结果的演算进行饱和；(2) 将剩余特征（名义、数量限制、角色公理）在活跃域上实例化。然后利用SDD的基于证据条件的加权模型计数（WMC）训练感知网络，在部分ABox监督下识别真实图像。针对推理捷径，提出以查询的理由（justifications）为索引的混合分布来表征校准后验，并从电路枚举的完备集中播种该混合。

Result: 在一个覆盖全部独有$\mathcal{SROIQ}$特征的本体上，CNN能够学习读取MNIST数字并通过后继关系耦合，恢复出独立感知仅在随机水平上得到的潜在本体概念。在监督允许多个本体一致的完备集时，独立感知会坍缩到其中一个，形成推理捷径；而基于理由混合的方法能达到独立感知无法达到的校准后验，并在真实MNIST任务上超越单次WMC和BEARS-ensemble假设类，取得贝叶斯最优后验。编译器和表示结果已通过Lean 4机器验证。

Conclusion: Baobab首次在非Horn描述逻辑（$\mathcal{SROIQ}$）中实现了保持经典蕴含语义的神经符号学习，并系统地刻画和缓解了推理捷径问题。理由索引的混合表示提供了独立感知无法表达的校准后验能力，证明其方法在真实图像任务上达到贝叶斯最优。该工作为在表达能力丰富的知识库上进行可靠的神经符号推理提供了新的理论和方法基础，局限性在于仍需在更大规模本体上验证可扩展性。

Abstract: OWL 2 DL ontologies, grounded in the description logic $\mathcal{SROIQ}$, express large knowledge bases in biomedicine and the Semantic Web. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learners over description logics either embed the ontology in a continuous space, abandoning classical entailment, or restrict to the Horn fragment $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$, which has a single canonical model. We present Baobab, which compiles a $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ ontology with a finite ABox into a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD): it saturates a propositional core under a consequence-based calculus and instantiates the remaining $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ features (nominals, number restrictions, and the role axioms) over the active domain. The SDD's evidence-conditioned weighted model count then trains a perception network to recognize real images under partial ABox supervision: on an ontology that exercises every distinctive $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ feature, a CNN learns to read MNIST digits coupled by a successor relation and recovers latent ontology concepts that an independent perception leaves at chance. When the supervision admits several ontology-consistent completions, an independent perception collapses onto one, a reasoning shortcut: we show that a mixture indexed by the query's justifications can represent the calibrated posterior no independent perception can, and that seeding it from the circuit's enumerated completions attains the Bayes-optimal posterior on a real-image MNIST task where single-WMC and learned mixtures (the BEARS-ensemble hypothesis class) do not: to our knowledge the first to characterize and mitigate reasoning shortcuts in a non-Horn description logic. Soundness of the compiler and the representation result are machine-checked in Lean 4. Code is available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/baobab.

</details>


### [147] [The Curious Case of Exploding DecPOMDPs: Containing the Fire through Policy Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17749)
*Nazlı Nur Karabulut,tanya Braun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出策略计数DecPOMDP框架，将对称性利用从"计数智能体"转向"计数策略"，从而实现智能体数量上的可解性，并设计了基于紧凑表示的策略计数动态规划算法进行高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DecPOMDP是建模多智能体不确定性决策的通用框架，但在智能体数量上呈指数级复杂度。现有方法通过利用智能体间的对称性（通过计数）来压缩模型，但策略空间仍然爆炸，模型复杂度和评估代价虽降至多项式依赖，策略求解仍不可行。因此需要探索新的对称性利用途径。

Method: 本文将对称性利用的视角从"计数智能体"转向"计数策略"，定义策略计数DecPOMDP，其中策略作为计数对象来实现智能体数量上的可解性。在此基础上提出策略计数动态规划算法，利用紧凑表示高效求解策略计数DecPOMDP。

Result: 所提出的策略计数DecPOMDP框架及策略计数动态规划算法实现了对具有特定对称性的多智能体决策问题在智能体数量维度上的高效求解。

Conclusion: 通过转换对称性编码的粒度（从计数智能体到计数策略），有效规避了策略空间爆炸问题，为大规模多智能体协同决策提供了一种新的可解途径。

Abstract: Decentralised partially observable Markov decision processes (DecPOMDPs) provide a general framework for modelling multi-agent decision making under uncertainty. However, DecPOMDPs are known to suffer from exponential complexity in the number of agents. One way to combat this intractability in agent numbers is to look at partitions of agents that exhibit a form of symmetry among agents, allowing for a compact encoding by counting. However, a challenge arises as the policy space explodes, even though the model complexity and evaluation cost reduce to a polynomial dependence. In this paper, we redirect our focus from counting agents to counting policies, which actually enables tractability in agent numbers for so called policy-counted DecPOMDPs. Further, we present policy-counted dynamic programming using the compact representation to solve policy-counted DecPOMDPs efficiently.

</details>


### [148] [D$^2$ACCI: A Dual-Loop Diagnostic Protocol for Evidence-Preserving Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17756)
*Xule Liu,Yijun Liu,Chao Li,Shao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出D²ACCI协议，这是一个用于LLM智能体持久记忆系统的双循环诊断驱动迭代协议，配合DCR可观测性度量与D²ACCI-Eval可复用工件，帮助定位多阶段记忆流水线（摄取、检索、过滤、生成）中的故障环节，并通过配对统计检验、受保护切片监控和追踪级可定位性来评估和迭代记忆干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的持久记忆涉及多阶段流水线（摄取、检索、过滤、生成），但现有端到端评估只能说明错误发生，无法定位是哪个阶段导致的。同时，现有评估往往仅报告聚合性能，缺乏配对统计比较、切片级非回归检查和阶段级诊断追踪，导致记忆系统迭代难以基于可靠证据进行。

Method: 提出D²ACCI双循环协议：外层诊断门控根据配对证据、受保护切片监控和追踪级可定位性来晋升（promote）、特性标记（feature-flag）或拒绝（reject）记忆干预。引入DCR（分等级可观测性度量）衡量故障是否保持可定位，以及D²ACCI-Eval可复用工件用于门控重放。将该协议实例化为MemStack系统。

Result: 在三个公开基准上验证（LoCoMo 93.59%、LongMemEval 90.93%、PersonaMem-V2 57.20%）。五个配对消融实验表明补充提取、会话记忆检索和Forget Guard带来统计显著提升（+1.9至+3.7pp，所有p≤.003）。BM25/RRF作为受监控特性标记保留，此决定性差异在仅聚合评估中不可见。诊断审计显示富集追踪显著提升根因一致性；诊断工件达到98-100% DCR@3，而仅结果日志为0%。

Conclusion: 稳健的记忆系统迭代需要可追踪、统计上有依据且具备回归感知的证据。现有评估的聚合式报告无法满足这一需求，D²ACCI恰好填补了该空白，证明了诊断驱动的迭代协议相较于传统结果导向评估的显著优势。

Abstract: Memory is a key capability of LLM agents. Persistent memory extends this across sessions---enabling recall, revision, and personalization. Yet its multi-stage pipeline (ingestion, retrieval, filtering, generation) makes failures difficult to localize: end-to-end evaluation reveals that an error occurred, but not which stage caused it. Existing evaluations often report aggregate performance without paired statistical comparisons, slice-level non-regression checks, or stage-level diagnostic traces. We propose D$^2$ACCI (Diagnostic-Driven Artifact-based Closed-loop Controlled Iteration), a dual-loop protocol whose outer diagnostic gate promotes, feature-flags, or rejects memory interventions based on paired evidence, protected-slice monitoring, and trace-level localizability. We further introduce DCR, a graded observability metric that measures whether failures remain localizable, and D$^2$ACCI-Eval, a reusable artifact for gate replay. We instantiate the protocol in MemStack and evaluate on three public benchmarks, achieving 93.59% on LoCoMo, 90.93% on LongMemEval, and 57.20% on PersonaMem-V2. Five paired ablations show that supplement extraction, session-memory retrieval, and Forget Guard yield statistically significant gains (+1.9 to +3.7pp, all p $\le$ .003). In contrast, BM25/RRF is retained as a monitored feature flag---a distinction invisible to aggregate-only evaluation. A diagnostic audit shows enriched traces substantially improve root-cause agreement over result-only relabeling. Diagnostic artifacts reach 98--100% DCR@3 versus 0% for results-only logs. These results establish that robust memory-system iteration demands traceable, statistically grounded, and regression-aware evidence---exactly the gap D$^2$ACCI fills.

</details>


### [149] [StartupBench: Benchmarking General-Purpose Agents on Market-Validated End-to-End Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17800)
*Liya Zhu,Xin Ma,Tao Liu,Haodong Wang,Ge Zhang,Jingzhe Ding,Qingshui Gu,Yongjie Zhong,Jinxiang Meng,Yuan Gao,Yunqiu Zhou,Hao Zhu,Jifeng He,Yongzhi Liao,Xinyi Zhang,Chaoxin Li,Yi Zhu,Xi Lin,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Wen Zhang,Yunyang Wang,Duo Wang,Huan Zhou,Zuo Wang,Jin Chen,Kaiyuan Zhang,Chuqian Yu,Tianhao Yu,Longxiang Liu,Jianbo Xue,Huimin Che,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StartupBench，一个基于市场验证的AI初创产品工作流的端到端智能体基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在真实用户需求上的完成能力。评测发现最强模型也仅能完成约30%的任务，揭示了当前通用智能体在复杂指令遵循和领域专业知识方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多依赖研究者人为挑选的任务，无法准确反映AI系统是否真正满足现实世界中用户的实际需求。为了测量LLM智能体在真实市场验证过的工作流上的端到端表现，需要一个基于实际采用的产品和用户工作流构建的基准。

Method: 作者系统性地研究具有市场验证的AI初创产品及其产品工作流和用户，从中提取真实的交付导向型任务。通过细致的评分标准评估任务的复杂需求，并在统一的智能体框架下对多个代表性模型进行评估。

Result: 即使最强模型也仅能成功完成约30%的StartupBench任务，尽管在多数任务上有部分进展。进一步分析指出复杂指令遵循和领域特定专业知识是主要的失败来源。

Conclusion: 大量市场验证的工作流超出了当前通用智能体的可靠能力范围，StartupBench可作为衡量向端到端完成真实用户任务进展的实证基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models(LLMs) and agents have substantially improved the ability of AI systems to execute complex tasks. Yet existing benchmarks largely rely on researcher-selected tasks, leaving uncertain whether such progress extends to the work that real-world users actually demand from AI systems. We introduce \textbf{StartupBench}, an E2E agent benchmark grounded in market-validated AI startup products. Rather than defining tasks from pre-defined assumptions about useful agent capabilities, we systematically study AI products with demonstrated adoption, together with their product workflows and users, to identify real-world tasks for which AI has established practical demand across diverse professional domains. We translate these workflows into complete deliverable-oriented tasks and evaluate them with fine-grained rubrics capturing their complex requirements. Across representative models evaluated under a unified agent harness, even the strongest model successfully completes only approximately 30\% of StartupBench, despite making substantial partial progress on many tasks. Further analysis identifies aspects like complex instruction following and domain-specific expertise as major sources of failure. Our results reveal that many market-validated workflows remain beyond the reliable capabilities of current general-purpose agents, establishing StartupBench as an empirical measure of progress toward E2E completions of real-world user tasks.

</details>


### [150] [ARASH: Adaptive Retrieval And Shot Selection for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17856)
*Samirasadat Jamalidinan,Yue Xu,Kazem Cheshmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARASH方法，通过基于训练集中局部邻域分析的自适应查询特定检索与示例选择策略，为TabPFN等表格基础模型筛选最优的in-context learning示例（shots），在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅降低提示长度和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格预测任务中，Tabular Foundation Models（如TabPFN）虽性能优异但需要大量计算资源且频繁重训不切实际。In-context learning（少样本提示）是资源高效的替代方案，但如何从训练集中挑选最相关的示例行作为shots仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 提出ARASH（Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection）方法，通过分析训练集中的局部邻域信息，为每个查询自适应地选择最优的示例（shots），从而提升TFM的推理效率。

Result: ARASH在保持与TabPFN相当精度的前提下，将提示长度缩减1261.5倍，内存使用量降低2.56倍。

Conclusion: ARASH通过查询特定的自适应示例检索与选择，有效解决了表格数据中ICL示例选择难题，显著提升了TabPFN的资源效率，为表格领域的基础模型部署提供了轻量级且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction is a critical task across numerous applications. The recent success of large language models has sparked various approaches for adapting them to the tabular domain. A prevalent strategy involves training or fine-tuning specialized Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) such as TabPFN. However, TFMs require substantial computational resources, and frequent model retraining is often impractical. In-context learning (ICL), specifically, few-shot prompting, offers a resource-efficient alternative to enhance performance. Yet, identifying the most relevant rows to serve as shots remains a challenge for tabular data. This paper introduces ARASH (Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection), a method that improves TFM efficiency by selecting optimal shots based on local neighborhood analysis within the training set. Our results demonstrate that ARASH reduces the prompt length and memory usage of TabPFN by 1261.5$\times$ and 2.56$\times$, respectively, while providing comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [151] [AutoResearch: Insight In, Hallucination Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17906)
*Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Qumeng Sun,Xiao Zhang,Jiahao Li,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoResearch是一个两阶段自主研究系统，将"Idea Generation"（想法生成）与"Idea Execution"（想法执行）相连接，通过多模型生成与交叉审查产生可测试的研究计划，并由协调代理分解执行实验、进行独立基于证据的审查后再接受研究结论。在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动机器学习等多个场景中，该系统能产生可衡量的进展、检测并纠正不可靠的实验结果，并做出基于证据的继续、修改或终止决策。在RSICD基准上，自动生成的idea将平均Recall从32.84提升至34.69，且仅记录5次审计确认的问题事件（而其他自主研究系统为11-27次）。核心主张是"Insight In, Hallucination Out"——洞察先于实验被夯实，结论先于接受被夯实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统虽然能执行长时程研究工作流，但纯粹的自动化并不能保证研究过程在科学上是严谨的。已有的自主研究系统缺乏对研究想法形成过程的科学基础把关，也缺乏对实验结论可靠性的独立验证机制，容易产生幻觉式或未经充分验证的研究结论。

Method: AutoResearch采用两阶段架构：1）Idea Generation阶段，持续整合新兴研究信号与累积的领域知识，识别可迁移的机制性见解，通过多模型生成和交叉审查产出有根据的、可测试的研究计划；2）Idea Execution阶段，协调智能体将计划分解为实验，迭代实现与诊断，并在接受研究结论前进行独立的基于证据的审查。

Result: 在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动机器学习等代表性设置中，AutoResearch将生成的Idea转化为可衡量的进展，能检测并纠正不可靠的实验结果。在RSICD基准上，生成的idea将平均Recall从32.84提升至34.69，且仅记录了5次审计确认的问题事件，而其他自主研究系统为11-27次。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了自主研究过程可以实现双重"grounded"——实验前的洞察夯实与接受前的结论夯实，从而显著降低幻觉式研究输出的风险，为自主科研系统提供了"Insight In, Hallucination Out"这一更可靠的研究范式。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are increasingly capable of executing long research workflows, yet automation alone does not ensure that the resulting process remains scientifically grounded. We introduce AutoResearch, a two-stage system that connects Idea Generation with Idea Execution to address both how research ideas are formed and how they are reliably established through experimentation. In Idea Generation, AutoResearch continuously integrates emerging research signals with accumulated domain knowledge, identifies transferable mechanistic insights, and uses multi-model generation and cross-review to produce grounded, testable research plans. In Idea Execution, coordinated agents decompose these plans into experiments, iteratively implement and diagnose them, and employ independent evidence-based review before accepting research conclusions. Across representative settings in cross-modal retrieval, systems optimization, and benchmark-driven machine learning, AutoResearch turns generated ideas into measurable progress, detects and corrects unreliable experimental results, and makes evidence-conditioned decisions to continue, revise, or terminate research directions. For example, on RSICD benchmark, an AutoResearch-generated idea improves mean Recall from 32.84 to 34.69, while recording only 5 audit-confirmed issue events compared with 11-27 for other autonomous research systems. These results demonstrate a research process in which meaningful insight is grounded before experimentation and conclusions are grounded before acceptance: Insight In, Hallucination Out.

</details>


### [152] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的自适应策略组合问题：离线合成有限个无记忆随机化策略，并通过轻量级在线选择器适配未知但固定的动态环境。提出了"鲁棒遗憾"作为组合质量的度量，并给出了组合认证与合成的复杂性理论结果（分别是∀ℝ-完备和∃∀ℝ-完备），同时提出了一种可运行时特化的离线组合构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RMDP优化单个策略来应对一组转移函数，但当未知动态是固定的且部署后部分可辨识时，这种方式可能过于保守。论文希望利用不确定性在部署后部分消解的特点，通过策略组合加在线选择的方式降低保守性，而非一开始就生成单一稳健策略。

Method: 形式化定义了自适应策略组合（有限无记忆随机化策略集合+在线选择器）的在线/离线框架，以鲁棒遗憾度量组合质量。从复杂性理论角度刻画了组合认证（∀ℝ-完备）和组合合成（∃∀ℝ-完备）的计算复杂度，分析了确定性组合、无环矩形RMDP、一般有理多胞体等设定，并构造了可运行时特化的离线组合算法。

Result: （1）在无环(s,a)-矩形RMDP中，对于确定性组合，组合认证问题是∀ℝ-完备的；（2）对于一般有理多胞体，合成一元有界大小的组合是∃∀ℝ-完备的，即使折扣固定且动态无环；（3）单策略情形在组合和代数意义上都已经是困难的；（4）提出了支持运行时特化的离线组合构建框架。

Conclusion: 策略组合+在线选择器为RMDP提供了一种在部署后利用部分可辨识性以降低保守性的可行范式，但其底层组合认证与合成问题在最坏情况下具有极高的计算复杂性（跨越实数层次），仅靠精确算法难以求解，需依赖能满足运行时要求的构造性方法。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [153] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EvoTS-Agent，一个基于验证引导的自我进化LLM智能体，用于自主金融时间序列变点检测。通过探索性数据分析初始化模型，并利用Revision、Alternative Strategy和Recombination三个算子进化实验轨迹，在四个基准数据集上优于现有LLM智能体，且在所有评估的后端LLM上保持100%的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性和异质性统计特性，单一无监督算法无法在所有资产和市场制度下表现一致，导致变点检测中高度依赖专家驱动的模型选择、特征设计与超参数调优，限制了可扩展性和适应性。

Method: EvoTS-Agent首先执行受控的探索性数据分析以刻画数据集特性并初始化候选检测模型；随后通过三个互补算子进化可执行的实验轨迹：Revision利用当前最优解，Alternative Strategy在进展停滞时探索根本不同的建模方向，Recombination综合高性能轨迹的互补证据。验证反馈在整个搜索过程中引导轨迹进化，使检测流程自适应每个数据集的同时保持可靠的优化。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，EvoTS-Agent持续优于现有的基于LLM的智能体，并在所有评估的后端LLM上保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: EvoTS-Agent通过验证引导的自我进化机制，实现了无需人工干预的自主金融时间序列变点检测，在保持执行可靠性的前提下有效适应了不同数据集的统计特征。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [154] [Procedural Content Metageneration via Program Search and Continual Abstraction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17947)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出持续抽象发现（CAD）方法，用于在程序生成器进化搜索过程中从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语。在四个游戏域（Sokoban、Zelda、Dangerous Dave、Lode Runner）中，CAD在全部八个域与API对比组合中均提升了平均最终适应度，证明了可复用原语发现能改善内容生成器的进化程序搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成可执行程序，使得可以直接搜索过程化内容生成器而非单个关卡层级。然而，进化搜索程序生成器的效率受限于缺乏可复用的构建模块，每次运行都从零开始搜索。作者希望通过从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语来加速进化搜索过程。

Method: 提出Continual Abstraction Discovery (CAD)方法。在每一代进化中，从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到一个运行专属的辅助模块中。实验采用2x2设计，交叉比较CAD（有/无）与固定手写领域API（有/无）四种条件，在四个游戏域中进行至少10次50代运行，共计160次完整运行。程序通过语言模型的变异与交叉操作完成进化。

Result: CAD在全部八个域与API对比组合中均提高了平均最终最佳适应度。在所有CAD运行中，学习到的库被大多数后续程序采用，并且重复发现了验证、可达性和结构性等实用工具函数。完成的实验数据集包含160次完整运行。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在进化程序搜索内容生成器的过程中，发现可复用原语能够显著提升搜索性能。CAD方法验证了持续积累抽象知识对程序进化的价值，这一思路可推广到更广泛的程序生成与进化计算任务中。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable programs, which makes it possible to search directly over procedural content generators rather than individual levels. We study this approach in Sokoban, Zelda, Dangerous Dave, and Lode Runner. Each run evolves complete Python generators through language-model mutation and crossover. We introduce Continual Abstraction Discovery, or CAD, which extracts reusable primitives from high-fitness programs into a run-specific helper module. A 2x2 experiment crosses CAD with access to a fixed hand-written domain API. The completed data set contains 160 complete runs, with at least ten 50-generation runs in every cell. CAD raises mean final best fitness in all eight domain and API comparisons. Across all CAD runs, learned libraries are adopted by most later programs and repeatedly rediscover validation, reachability, and structural utilities. These results support that discovering reusable primitives improves evolutionary program search for content generators.

</details>


### [155] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号世界模型（neurosymbolic world model），通过将奖励预测与观测重构解耦，使奖励仅依赖于潜在状态中的结构化符号子集，从而实现在无需额外环境交互的情况下零样本泛化到新的奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型强化学习方法学习神经网络世界模型，虽然表达能力强，但所学到的潜在表征是任务依赖的、不可解释的，难以泛化到新任务。作者希望解决世界模型在新任务上的泛化问题，使模型能够适应定义在同一符号状态空间上的新奖励函数。

Method: 提出一种新颖的世界模型公式：将奖励预测与观测重构解耦，使奖励预测仅依赖于潜在状态中的一组结构化、符号化组件。观测重构负责建模环境动态，而符号状态组件负责奖励预测，从而实现任务无关的表征学习。

Result: 该方法在零样本设置下，即无需进一步环境交互，即可适应定义在相同符号状态空间上的新奖励函数；实验证明该方法在泛化性能上优于纯神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号世界模型通过解耦观测重构与奖励预测，利用结构化符号状态组件实现了更强的跨任务泛化能力，为基于模型的强化学习的泛化问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [156] [Can Large Language Models Explain Flight Safety Events? A Prior-Guided Semantic LLM-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18017)
*Lu Xu,Xu Li,Linjiang Zheng,Fan Li,Riquan Zhang,Jiaxing Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FlightLLM，一种基于先验引导语义LLM的可解释飞行安全分析方法，通过特征工程解决模态不一致、语义离散化模块将数值模式转换为定性描述、引入CatBoost作为统计专家提供先验引导、采用对比少样本学习弥补数据不足，并在重着陆任务上于704个真实A320航班样本上验证，实现了竞争性分类性能与可解释性兼备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行安全分析不仅需要准确检测风险事件，更重要的是在飞行员控制行为层面清晰解释其潜在原因。现有可解释AI技术（如特征重要性图）需要大量领域知识才能转化为可操作的解释，而LLM擅长语言推理，为这一难题提供了有前景的解决方案。但LLM在飞行安全领域的应用面临模态不一致、分类能力有限、微调数据稀缺及缺乏领域知识等关键挑战。

Method: FlightLLM采用多组件管线：(1)特征工程：组合统计描述符与物理意义的飞行指标，解决模态不一致；(2)语义离散化模块：将抽象数值模式转换为定性描述，与语言推理兼容；(3)集成CatBoost作为统计专家，将其预测结果注入提示词作为先验引导，弥补LLM分类能力不足；(4)对比少样本学习策略应对数据有限问题；(5)设计结构化提示词嵌入航空领域知识到推理过程。

Result: 以重着陆这一具有复杂因果机制的代表性风险事件为锚点，在包含704个真实A320航班样本的数据集上评估FlightLLM。实验结果表明，该方法在实现具有竞争力分类性能的同时，能够生成直接且合理的事件原因解释。

Conclusion: FlightLLM通过将LLM的语言推理能力与统计模型的分类优势相结合，并借助语义离散化、先验引导和领域知识嵌入，成功解决了LLM在飞行安全分析领域的四大挑战，实现了分类性能与可解释性之间的良好平衡，为基于飞行数据的可解释安全分析提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Improving flight safety with flight data requires not only accurate detection of risk events, but more importantly, clear interpretation of their underlying causes at the level of pilot control behavior. Existing explainable AI techniques, such as feature importance maps, often require considerable domain knowledge to translate them into operationally meaningful explanations. Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [157] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StagedWorkspace，一个面向AI知识工作智能体的版本化工作区系统。该系统通过内容哈希将解析记录和审查差异（diffs）绑定到原生文件的版本，解决了智能体在搜索、编辑、审查和提交过程中面对同一工作产品的不同版本的问题。实验表明，双重解析/原生访问方式在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents上均优于单一视图，显著提升各模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地执行知识工作（生成和修改代码仓库、文档、电子表格、幻灯片、报告等持久化数字工件），但智能体搜索时解析的视图、编辑的原生文件、审查的变更以及提交的工件可能引用同一工作产品的不同版本。编码智能体通过"仓库契约"（repository contracts）部分解决了搜索、差异和测试的版本一致性问题，而对PDF、电子表格、幻灯片、笔记本和混合格式项目文件夹而言，类似的契约不够明确。因此需要一种版本化工作区来显式地将每个视图绑定到工作区状态的具体版本。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace系统，一个为知识工作智能体设计的版本化工作区。核心机制是：工作区将解析后的记录（parsed records）和审查差异（review diffs）通过内容哈希绑定到原生文件的变化状态。具体包括：(1) 双重视图访问——同时提供解析视图和原生文件访问；(2) 版本绑定——每次解析或diff都与特定文件内容哈希关联；(3) 使用固定测试框架（fixed-harness）在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents两个基准上进行消融实验，并通过配对审查轴消融（paired review-axis ablation）在57个文件编辑任务上验证diff可见性的影响。

Result: 在固定框架消融实验中，双重解析/原生访问在所有测试模型上均获得最高的点估计值；相比单一视图限制，OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1个百分点，APEX平均评分提升4.7-9.2个百分点。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上使用Gemini 3.1 Pro达到63.9%（对比发布同模型分数29.3%），在APEX上使用GPT-5.4 Nano达到42.1（对比25.5）。57个文件编辑任务的配对审查轴消融进一步发现，当diff可见时观测得分更高。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，工作区状态（workspace state）应被视为知识工作智能体中一个独立的实验变量。本文推动了在基准测试中对证据（evidence）、分阶段编辑（staged edits）和提交工件（submitted artifacts）进行评分，将它们视为显式的状态转换（explicit state transitions），从而更好地评估和提升知识工作智能体的能力。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [158] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HLSR，一种选择性混合实时-预测车辆重路由框架，在受限干预范围下融合实时边缘速度与短期预测，通过多重机制提升交通拥堵缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全网实时最短路径重路由在仿真中效果显著，但假设每辆车上路车辆在每个决策周期都被重新规划，实际部署中难以实现。需要一种在有限干预范围内仍能高效缓解拥堵的重路由方法。

Method: HLSR框架包含：双阈值拥堵检测、校准上游选择和驾驶员定制旅行时间预测；并引入接近车辆扩展、旅行时间加权k最短路径生成、以及依赖时间范围的混合实时-预测路段速度用于多成本路径分配。

Result: 通过选择性重路由与混合实时-预测信息的结合，在受限干预条件下实现接近全网重路由的拥堵缓解效果。

Conclusion: HLSR证明在有限的车辆干预范围内，通过融合实时与预测信息并引入多种路径生成与分配策略，可以有效缓解城市交通拥堵、降低出行成本与排放。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [159] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两项大规模调查（总计N=5,511，覆盖两种语言）研究用户在约会平台上对自主LLM代理的接受度。作者建立了基于分级响应模型与潜在回归的代理接受度测量模型，发现发送与接收代理通信的意愿是高度相关（rho=0.92）但可分离（ΔBIC=52）的不同构念，并揭示了系统性的委托不对称性：部署自身代理所需接受度阈值（-0.38）远低于参与对方代理（+0.32，完全参与+1.39），部署倾向约为参与倾向的三倍。在随机配对的反事实模拟中，仅4-13%的有向二元组同时满足代理部署与接收参与，且存在显著的性别方向性失衡。设计反事实分析表明：互惠要求可使交互量减半以上，而基于接收接受度路由代理联系人可使单次接触参与度提升三倍（AUC 0.88，交叉验证下3.1倍四分位数提升）。文章讨论了代理推荐系统设计启示，包括披露、选择机制与接受度感知的匹配算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理代表用户对话正在成为匹配平台的新兴设计模式，但这一模式是否可行取决于一个很少被检验的前提：用户不仅要接受将对话委托给代理，还必须接受接收来自他人代理的中介通信。本研究的动机正是检验这一关键前提是否成立。

Method: 采用两项大型调查（生成式个人资料特征N=2,894；自主对话代理N=2,617，两种语言），开发了基于分级响应模型（graded response model）和潜变量回归的代理接受度测量模型，通过模型比较检验发送与接收意愿的构念区分性，并进行跨语言测量不变性检验；基于陈述性接受度构建随机配对反事实，评估设计反事实对交互量和参与度的影响。

Result: 发送和接收代理通信意愿是高度相关但可分离的构念；存在系统性委托不对称（部署阈值-0.38 vs 参与阈值+0.32/完全参与+1.39）；随机配对下仅4-13%的定向二元组能同时部署与接收；互惠要求排除近三分之二部署使交互量减半以上；接收接受度驱动的路由将单次接触参与度提升三倍，且通过以目标项留出为条件的样本外验证（AUC 0.88）。

Conclusion: 代理接受度是匹配平台自主代理可行性的关键瓶颈，委托不对称和性别方向失衡意味着平台不能仅关注让用户部署自身代理，还必须解决接收端接受度问题。设计上可通过互惠约束、面向接收接受度的路由、披露与选择机制以及接受度感知的匹配算法来优化整体交互效率。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>


### [160] [On the Fragility of Self-Improving Agents: Variance, Task Order, and Underspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18066)
*Qinyuan Ye,Yu Li,Yada Pruksachatkun,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了基于记忆的自我改进型智能体，发现其评估存在固有噪声且改进高度依赖任务顺序，并揭示任务与环境欠规格化是导致脆弱性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型自我改进智能体在近期文献中展现出巨大潜力，但其可靠性方面被严重忽视。作者旨在揭示这些方法在更广泛评估条件下的脆弱性。

Method: 对两种基于记忆的方法进行重新评估，沿两个维度扩展评估范围：(1) 多次运行以量化方差；(2) 随机打乱任务顺序以研究任务顺序的影响。进一步人工检查智能体记忆并提出欠规格化假设，通过向记忆构建过程注入更详细的评分标准和环境反馈来验证该假设。

Result: 发现两个核心问题：智能体评估在复杂环境多步任务中固有噪声大，自我改进循环会进一步放大噪声；智能体的改进高度依赖任务顺序，先前的默认顺序隐含了一种隐性课程。注入规格化信息仅部分弥合性能差距，仍有显著缺口存在。

Conclusion: 呼吁更严格的评估协议（多轮运行结果报告及压力测试），并倡导构建支持有效人类监督的系统与接口，防止智能体以不可预见的方式失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [VLCP: Vision Language Control Policy Closed-Loop Code Replanning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16978)
*Dhia Naouali,Minghan Wu,Claudia Wong,Abhinav Puthran,Omar G. Younis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLCP提出了一种无需微调、无需演示的机器人策略方法：保持视觉-语言模型（VLM）冻结，让它编写短小的Python控制函数作为策略。关键创新在于闭环控制——每隔K步让VLM重新观察场景并重写控制代码，从而在单次episode内修复失败（尤其是失败抓取）。在57个MuJoCo/RoboVerse任务上达到35.1%的池化成功率，比单次查询的基线（3.5%）提升约十倍，且约84%的输入token命中缓存，成本低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前沿视觉-语言模型转化为机器人策略的常见做法是微调它以输出从未在预训练中见过的动作表示，这会丢掉模型原本有价值的推理能力。作者反其道而行，保持VLM冻结，让它以代码形式编写策略。但一次性编写的代码属于开环控制，现有闭环方法在错误的层面反应——要么重试固定策略，要么选择不同子任务，从未重写失败的代码本身。因此需要一种在正确层面（控制代码）闭环的方法。

Method: VLCP保持VLM完全冻结，由VLM编写Python控制函数作为策略，不依赖演示数据或微调。在每次episode内，每隔K步VLM从多视角RGB、本体感知状态和状态增量重新观察场景，并根据观察结果重写控制函数。重写发生在控制代码层面，使失败在恶化之前就被捕获。跨episode时，重写过程中生成的控制代码块可存入技能库，供后续提示复用。

Result: 在57个MuJoCo/RoboVerse任务上，训练-free策略达到35.1%的池化成功率，而相同系统单次查询每episode仅有3.5%，两者差距约十倍且在每个场景族中置信区间不重叠。增益源于27.3%的episode内失败抓取恢复率——开环控制器会带至episode结束的失误可被重新观察并在下一次重规划时修复。效率方面，中位数84%的输入token命中缓存，每次episode仅需约10次紧凑查询。

Conclusion: VLCP证明了一个关键洞察：闭环应该在失败实际发生的地方——控制代码层面——进行，而非在任务或策略选择层面。通过在单次episode内重写控制函数，可以让冻结的VLM既保留预训练的推理能力，又获得远超开环基线的鲁棒性，同时保持较低的计算成本，并为跨episode的技能积累提供了途径。

Abstract: Turning a frontier vision-language model into a robot policy usually means fine-tuning it to emit an action representation it never saw in pretraining, which throws away much of the reasoning that made the model worth reaching for. We go the other way and keep the VLM frozen. It writes the policy as a short Python control function, with no demonstrations and no fine-tuning. Writing that code once is open-loop, though. Existing closed-loop methods react at the wrong level: they retry a fixed policy or pick a different subtask, but never rewrite the code that failed. VLCP closes the loop where the failure actually lives, on the control code, within a single episode. Every $K$ steps the VLM re-observes the scene from multi-view RGB, proprioceptive state, and a state delta, then rewrites the control function from what it just saw, so a failure is caught before it compounds.
  We evaluate on a 57-task MuJoCo/RoboVerse sweep. This training-free policy reaches $35.1\%$ pooled success, against $3.5\%$ for the identical system queried once per episode. That tenfold gap holds with non-overlapping confidence intervals in every scene family. The gain traces to a $27.3\%$ within-episode recovery rate on failed grasps: a miss an open-loop controller would carry to the end of the episode gets re-observed and fixed at the next replan. And the loop stays cheap. A median $84\%$ of input tokens hit cache, an episode needs only about $10$ compact queries, and control blocks written during any replan persist to a cross-episode skill library reused in later prompts.

</details>


### [162] [FetchMan: Learning Visual Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Policies from Simulated Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17027)
*Omar Rayyan,Zhi Li,Max Argus,Yuxin Jiang,Chang Yu,Chenfanfu Jiang,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FetchMan，一种端到端的sim-to-real流水线，通过合成数据行为克隆加Flow-GRPO强化学习微调，训练双足人形机器人的视觉定位操作（loco-manipulation）策略，可在零样本下部署到真实Unitree G1机器人上，实现跨场景单目标到达-抓取73.3%成功率，并初步拓展到多目标训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的视觉定位操作（同时行走和操作）难以收集足够真实世界演示数据，传统数据密集型算法难以应对；而学步领域通过模拟数据迁移到现实成功的做法尚未被复用到定位操作任务上。

Method: 提出三阶段端到端sim-to-real流水线：(1) 在超过15万个仿真场景中合成演示数据；(2) 对合成演示进行行为克隆（BC）；(3) 在单一稀疏奖励下用Flow-GRPO强化学习微调克隆策略，突破行为克隆的性能上限。训练得到FetchMan策略，并在仿真基准FetchMan-Bench上评估后进行真实零样本部署。

Result: 行为克隆的合成演示方法无论训练数据多少都存在低性能上限；Flow-GRPO强化学习微调突破该上限，性能远超行为克隆。FetchMan在真实Unitree G1上零样本部署，单目标到达-抓取在未见场景中成功率73.3%。并拓展到多目标训练，向定位操作通用策略迈进一步。

Conclusion: 合成数据行为克隆+强化学习微调是可行的loco-manipulation训练范式，能从仿真迁移到真实世界实现零样本泛化；扩展到多目标训练是朝向定位操作通用策略的初步探索。

Abstract: Visual loco-manipulation policies that can generalize to novel scenes and objects have long been a goal of robotics research. However, today's data-hungry algorithms make collecting sufficient demonstrations a struggle for tabletop manipulation, and even more so for humanoids that must also walk and balance. Learning from simulated data and transferring that behavior to the real world, as is commonly done in locomotion, sidesteps this struggle, so we replicate that recipe for loco-manipulation. In doing so, we find that cloning synthetic demonstrations results in a low performance ceiling no matter the amount of training data. Reinforcement learning breaks through it, and refining the cloned policy with Flow-GRPO on a single sparse reward yields performance that synthetic behavior cloning cannot match. Together, these stages form our end-to-end sim-to-real pipeline spanning more than 150,000 scenes, which we use to train FetchMan. We evaluate it on FetchMan-Bench, a simulation benchmark we release, and deploy it zero-shot on a real Unitree G1, where our single-object reach-and-pick policy walks to and grasps a target across unseen scenes at 73.3% success. Finally, we extend this recipe to multi-object training, a first step toward loco-manipulation generalist policies at this data scale.

</details>


### [163] [Lambda-Hold Control: Human-Like Movement Emerges from a Minimal Task Reward in Predictive Musculoskeletal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17030)
*Jun Hyuk Lee,Chihyeong Lee,Jooeun Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出λ-hold控制器，受平衡点假设启发，将策略的控制变量改为每块肌肉的EP阈值长度λ，并通过拉伸反射机制自动计算肌肉兴奋度，同时大幅降低策略查询频率，首次使肌肉驱动的骨骼模型仅用一个小时训练和最小奖励便学会类人冲刺动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体肌肉骨骼系统的过度驱动导致动作空间高维且冗余，基于强化学习的训练中探索效率极低，难以生成类人运动。

Method: 受平衡点假设启发，提出λ-hold控制器：策略的控制变量为每块肌肉的EP阈值长度λ，通过拉伸反射招募律自动计算肌肉兴奋度；在步态相位区间内保持λ不变，大幅降低策略查询频率，从而提升探索效率。

Result: 首次使用肌肉驱动骨骼模型、仅需最小奖励和约一小时训练即可学习类人冲刺动作。

Conclusion: λ-hold控制器不仅是工程技巧，而是基于生理学的探索方法，融合了平衡点假设、间歇控制和最优反馈控制，为学习人类运动控制器提供了可训练模型。

Abstract: The massive overactuation in the human musculoskeletal system makes it challenging to train musculoskeletal models to generate human-like motion via reinforcement learning, primarily because exploration in the resulting high-dimensional and redundant action space is extremely inefficient. To address this problem, we propose the $λ$-hold controller, inspired by the equilibrium-point (EP) hypothesis, which has been widely supported by extensive evidence from human motor control studies. The policy's control variable is the per-muscle EP threshold length $λ$, from which a stretch-reflex recruitment law computes the muscle excitations automatically. Holding each $λ$ over an interval of the gait phase also sharply reduces the frequency at which the policy must be queried. Consequently, the controller, to our knowledge for the first time, enables a muscle-actuated skeletal model to learn human-like sprinting using only a minimal reward within an hour of training. The efficient exploration through the proposed $λ$-hold controller is not merely an engineering trick but an approach grounded in physiology, bringing together the EP hypothesis, intermittent control, and optimal feedback control. Beyond encapsulating human-like behavior in predictive simulation, this achievement contributes to developing a learnable model of the human motor controller.

</details>


### [164] [Terrain-Aware Local Path Planning with Global DEM Data Integration for Autonomous UGV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17038)
*Devender Singh,Issah Nazif Suleiman,Paul Mitten,Glenn Cutler,Vinicius Prado da Fonseca,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合框架，将低分辨率DEM数据与实时LiDAR感知相结合，通过基于DEM的A*全局路径规划与局部自适应修正策略，提升无人地面车辆在复杂户外地形中的自主导航能力，在Gazebo仿真中实现95%的障碍物避障率并将平均坡度从8°降至2.7°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂户外地形中，全局地图构建与实时传感器反馈之间存在固有脱节，导致无人地面车辆难以应对动态环境变化，现有方法在路径效率与地形可通行性之间缺乏有效平衡。

Method: 采用混合框架：首先利用预处理后的DEM数据运行A*算法获得全局路径，然后结合实时LiDAR障碍物检测与地形分析驱动局部路径自适应修正，使车辆能够应对突发环境变化。仿真验证在Gazebo环境中进行。

Result: 相比基线方法，所提框架在自定义地形中达到95%的障碍物避障率，并将车辆平均遭遇坡度从8°显著降低至2.7°，同时提升了路径效率与地形可通行性。

Conclusion: 全局-局部混合路径规划框架能有效结合离线地图信息与在线传感器反馈，在保障安全性的同时提升导航效率，为动态户外环境中更可靠的无人地面车辆自主导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in complex outdoor terrains presents critical challenges for unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) due to the inherent disconnect between global mapping and real-time sensor feedback. This work proposes a hybrid framework that integrates low-resolution Digital Elevation Model (DEM) data with real-time LiDAR-based obstacle detection and terrain analysis for efficient path planning. A global path is initially computed using a preprocessed DEM-based A* algorithm. Subsequently, local sensor data drives adaptive path correction, enabling the UGV to negotiate sudden environmental changes while maintaining safety and efficiency. Simulation results in Gazebo demonstrate significant improvements over a baseline approach, achieving a 95\% obstacle avoidance rate and reducing the average encountered slope from $8^\circ$ to $2.7^\circ$ in custom terrain. This integration enhances path efficiency and terrain traversability and supports robust real-time adaptation, paving the way for more reliable autonomous navigation in dynamic outdoor environments.

</details>


### [165] [PDDL-ART: Autonomous Symbolic Abstraction From Demonstration For Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17146)
*Disha Kamale,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PDDL-ART框架，利用视觉-语言模型从单一专家演示、自然语言任务描述和高层动作库中自动生成PDDL领域（domain）和问题（problem）描述，无需领域模板、动作签名或微调，通过多阶段校正（语法、语义、执行层面）确保生成质量，在执行层面引入符号谓词接地（symbolic predicate grounding），借助VLM的工具调用能力进行几何与时间推理，在发动机维护和家庭场景的挑战性操作任务中达到93.3%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL符号规划为长时程机器人操作提供了原则性框架，但手工构建准确的PDDL领域和问题描述需要大量领域专业知识，是当前的主要瓶颈。现有方法依赖领域模板、动作签名或微调，难以泛化到新任务。因此需要一种能自主生成PDDL描述且保证语法正确性与语义对齐的方法。

Method: PDDL-ART包含三个关键设计：(1) 从单个专家演示、自然语言任务描述和高层动作名库中生成PDDL领域与问题描述，无模板/签名/微调依赖；(2) 引入多阶段校正管线，分别在语法、语义和执行三个层面验证并修正生成的描述；(3) 在执行引导校正中，采用符号谓词接地机制，利用现代VLM的工具调用能力进行几何和时间推理，以评估无法仅从图像直接判别的关系谓词，且模型自主决定何时调用工具及如何解释输出。

Result: 在发动机维护和家庭领域的挑战性操作任务上评估，包括需要记忆、抽象谓词推断以及目标状态与初始状态视觉不可区分的任务。PDDL-ART平均成功率为93.3%，优于基线VLM规划器的78.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法证明VLM结合工具推理能够在无需领域专业知识输入的情况下自主生成高质量PDDL描述，显著优于传统基于VLM的直接规划方法，为符号规划与感知-运动层面的自动衔接提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Symbolic planning with PDDL offers a principled framework for long-horizon robot manipulation, but constructing accurate PDDL domain and problem descriptions remains a significant bottleneck, typically requiring substantial domain expertise. We present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based approach called PDDL-ART, a framework that autonomously generates task-specific PDDL domain and problem descriptions from a single expert demonstration, a natural language task description, and a library of available high-level action names. PDDL-ART does not require any domain templates, action signatures, or fine-tuning. To ensure the generated descriptions are not only syntactically valid but semantically aligned with the demonstrated task, PDDL-ART introduces a multi-stage correction pipeline operating at syntactic, semantic, and execution levels. A key component of execution-guided correction is symbolic predicate grounding. Instead of relying solely on visual observations, PDDL-ART leverages the tool-use capabilities of modern VLMs to incorporate geometric and temporal reasoning for evaluating relational predicates that are not directly discernible from images alone. Critically, the model autonomously determines when to invoke these tools and how to interpret their outputs. We evaluate PDDL-ART on challenging manipulation tasks in engine maintenance and household domains, including tasks that require memory, abstract predicate inference, and goal states that are visually indistinguishable from the initial state. PDDL-ART achieves an average success rate of 93.3%, compared to 78.3% for a baseline VLM-based planner.

</details>


### [166] [Iterative Grasp Pose Refinement: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for 2D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17628)
*Amir Arsalan Nematollahi,Shayan Ahmadi,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的机器人抓取优化框架，将关键点物体表示与深度Q网络（DQN）相结合，利用2D俯视图像生成初始抓取候选并通过迭代优化将失败抓取转化为成功抓取。在Dex-Net数据集的300个物体上使用UR5机械臂进行实验，实现了100%的成功率，并通过Delta并联机器人的物理实验验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够理解和操作物体的机器人需要紧凑、可解释且可泛化的表示。现有基于几何的方法在处理某些难以抓取的物体时存在局限，因此需要一种能够学习并优化抓取策略的框架，以提高抓取成功率并实现可迁移的解决方案。

Method: 该方法结合关键点物体表示与深度Q网络（DQN）构建强化学习框架。在模拟环境中，使用2D俯视图像，由一个基于几何的算法生成初始抓取候选，随后框架通过迭代优化逐步修正抓取动作，将失败的抓取转化为成功抓取。

Result: 在Dex-Net数据集的300个物体上使用UR5机械臂进行实验，框架对先前几何方法认为无法抓取的物体实现了100%的成功率。通过Delta并联机器人的物理实验进一步验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力，优化的抓取成功操控了先前无法抓取的物体。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，强化学习在机器人抓取中具有显著有效性，能够解决接触丰富的操作任务中的挑战，提供了一种可扩展且适应性强的解决方案。关键点表示与DQN的结合展现了在复杂抓取场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Developing robots capable of understanding and manipulating objects requires compact, interpretable, and generalizable representations. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-based framework for robotic grasp refinement, integrating keypoint-based object representations with a Deep Q-Network (DQN). Using 2D overhead images captured in a simulated environment, a geometric-based algorithm generates initial grasp candidates, which are iteratively refined by the proposed framework, transforming failed grasps into successful ones. Experiments conducted on 300 objects from the Dex-Net dataset using a UR5 manipulator demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, achieving a 100% success rate on objects previously deemed ungraspable by geometrical methods. The framework's sim-to-real transferability is further validated through physical experiments on a Delta parallel robot, where a refined grasp successfully manipulates an object that was previously ungraspable. The findings underscore the effectiveness of reinforcement learning in addressing challenges in robotic grasping, offering a scalable and adaptable solution for contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [167] [Teach and Grow: An Agent-Centered Architecture for General Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17209)
*Chang Nie,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Teach-and-Grow Learning (TGL)，一个以智能体为中心的通用机器人学习架构，通过将少量成功演示转化为可复用的Skill Blocks，在不进行任务特定策略重训练的情况下，实现新任务的获取与适应，并提出Teach-and-Grow缩放定律假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）与世界-动作模型虽然提供了通用机器人的优雅路径，但可靠性受限于验证过的物理覆盖范围。当遇到不熟悉的对象、传感器、具身形态或接触超出覆盖范围且无验证过的后备方案时，修正失败需要新机器人数据、策略更新和回归测试，这构成了持续的"重训练税"负担。与文本数据不同，具身数据通常必须通过操作机器来生成，成本高昂。

Method: TGL架构包含：(1) 多模态智能体将少量成功演示转化为Skill Blocks——针对有意义的子目标的可复现闭环行为；(2) 在新场景中，智能体对技能块进行接地与组合，选择学习的或几何工具，观察物理结果，并在执行偏离意图时修订路径；(3) Skill Library存储可执行行为，结构化的Experience Memory承载成功、失败和修复经验；(4) 新任务获取无需任务特定策略重训练。

Result: LIBERO评估达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能；对照研究展示了技能归纳、持续复用以及智能体导向的适应能力。

Conclusion: 提出Teach-and-Grow缩放定律假说：若X表示有效可复用经验，未来任务错误与教学需求应随着X的幂律逼近不可降低的下限。该架构将部署视为持续学习阶段，使一个任务能够使下一个任务更容易，从而缓解重训练税问题。

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models offer an elegant route to general-purpose robotics, but their reliability is bounded by validated physical coverage. When an unfamiliar object, sensor, embodiment, or contact falls outside that coverage and no validated fallback exists, correcting the failure requires new robot data, a policy update, and regression testing. This recurring burden is the retraining tax. Unlike text, embodied data must often be created by operating machines. We present Teach-and-Grow Learning (TGL), an agent-centered architecture for general robot learning. In its general form, a multimodal agent turns a few successful demonstrations into reusable Skill Blocks: closed-loop behaviors for meaningful subgoals. In a new scene, the agent grounds and composes these blocks, selects learned or geometric tools, observes the physical outcome, and revises the route when execution departs from intent. A Skill Library stores executable behavior, while structured Experience Memory carries forward success, failure, and repair. New tasks are acquired without task-specific policy retraining. Our LIBERO evaluation attains state-of-the-art performance; controlled studies expose skill induction, persistent reuse, and agent-directed adaptation. Finally, we propose the Teach-and-Grow scaling-law hypothesis: if X denotes effective reusable experience, future-task error and teaching demand should approach irreducible floors as power laws in X. The architecture therefore treats deployment as a period of continued learning, in which one task can make the next easier.

</details>


### [168] [Robust Brachiation on a Life-Sized Dual-Arm Robot Using Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17320)
*Ayumu Iwata,Kento Kawaharazuka,Keita Yoneda,Takahiro Hattori,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的航点引导强化学习（WGRL）框架，用于在真实尺寸的双臂机器人上实现越臂运动（brachiation），通过稀疏指定末端执行器航点引导行为习得，并结合任务成功与机械能奖励及Sim-to-Real训练环境，在几何变化的猴杠场景和硬件实验中验证了包括失败恢复在内的鲁棒运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越臂运动是灵长类动物仅靠手臂移动的 locomotion 方式，可在无立足点环境中通行，但该运动需要高度协调的全身动作与精确的抓杠/释放时机控制，在真实尺寸机器人平台上实现鲁棒行为极具挑战性。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的WGRL（Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning）框架：在缺乏模仿学习数据的高难度任务中，通过稀疏指定末端执行器轨迹航点来引导行为习得，全身运动由强化学习生成；将航点跟随引导与任务成功和机械能奖励相结合，并在面向Sim-to-Real迁移的环境中进行训练。

Result: 在具有几何变化的猴杠（monkey-bar）环境下通过Sim-to-Sim实验以及在真实硬件上通过硬件实验评估习得的行为，确认了包括失败恢复行为在内的鲁棒越臂运动能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为在真实尺寸机器人硬件上实现基于手臂的 locomotion 提供了有效的学习设计指南，并扩展了机器人的可通行工作空间。

Abstract: Brachiation is a form of locomotion in which primates move primarily using their arms, enabling traversal in environments without footholds. However, this motion requires highly coordinated whole-body movement and precise timing control for bar grasping and release. As a result, achieving robust behavior on life-sized robotic platforms remains challenging. In this study, we present a reinforcement learning-based method to realize brachiation on a life-sized dual-arm robot. The core of the proposed approach is Waypoint-Guided Reinforcement Learning (WGRL), a learning framework for inducing non-linear and complex motions. For high-difficulty tasks where imitation learning data are unavailable, WGRL guides behavior acquisition by sparsely specifying waypoints for the end-effector trajectory, while whole-body motion is generated through reinforcement learning. In addition, by integrating the waypoint-following guidance with rewards based on task success and mechanical energy, and training in an environment designed for Sim-to-Real transfer, the proposed method achieves both forward progression and motion stability.
